# Protocol requirements ledger

This is the implementation ledger for [the completion plan](protocol-completion-plan.md)
and [issue #18](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/issues/18).
The audit inventories the advertised profiles, outer schema fields, operation
subtypes and errors, binary controls, graph and synchronization structures.
The field tables below record gaps as well as implementation evidence. An
inventoried field with unresolved applicability or an absent acceptance test
remains open. This document does not establish exhaustive conformance or desktop
OneNote support. The audited implementation baseline is release commit
`b22bc2d29ec0cff61cd212c7d41fc68a297b3003`; parallel feature branches are not
counted as implemented until their changes and evidence land.

The profile/hashing update below uses merged main `8f1ac33` as its implementation
base, including #49/#50/#51/#52. Historical owner snapshots later in this document
remain provenance for those rounds. The finite matrix is the current decision
for advertised profiles; it does not convert unresolved field conditions into
passed acceptance tests.

Each row separates the wire requirement from CellBridge's implementation status.
A MUST within an operation defines its behavior when supported; it does not make
all optional protocol capabilities mandatory. Product exceptions describe the
named Microsoft products. They are evidence for a profile decision, not permission
to ignore every extension in every client version.

The profile/hashing validation on 2026-10-05 passed the full isolated runner,
including 824 .NET tests and 71 Python tests, with two platform-specific skips.
The runner used disposable PostgreSQL and HTTP hosts in this worktree. Independent
hash vectors and Microsoft decoding cover the bounded facility described below.
Native Astra reviewed the design before implementation and the material changes.
Raw reports remain under ignored `artifacts/testing/profile-hashing-full-4`.
These checks do not establish desktop or external-host qualification.

input subset. The inventories below enumerate outer operations, subtypes, scalar
fields, extension objects and selectors. This round resolves hashing applicability
and envelope validation. #18 remains open for the bounded field gates after this
table. Future Microsoft product profiles are outside this decision.

Outer unsupported operations emit `RequestNotSupported`; non-Success outer responses include ErrorMessage. Undecodable outer binary maps to Cell18 in the Cell subresponse. | Both executors, `CellBridgeRequestProcessor`; `CellBinaryRequestExecutorTests`, `BoundedProtocolMutationTests`, `FssHttpTests`, `CoreCorrectnessTests`, negotiated hash suites. | Implemented bounded classification and safe error text; independent operation-specific response acceptance remains R02/#18/#26. |

The selected profile has sixteen bounded decisions. A format name never establishes
full equivalence with a Microsoft product. The field inventories retain the exact
normative source for every inventoried field. Evidence without a field-specific
acceptance test stays open.

| Remaining gate | Bounded rows / required acceptance | Classification / issue |
| --- | --- | --- |
| R01 | V01 and core outer Cell attributes: required version/tokens/correlation/data shapes, XML Schema Boolean/integer lexical forms, GUIDs, timeout and BinaryDataSize ranges are preflighted for wire and direct execution. Supplied Etag is checked for reads and in final file/metadata transitions after receipt replay; stale requests do not mutate. Valid LastModifiedTime applies only to applicable successful new file commits. | Implemented synthetic gates in `FssHttpTests`, `AuthorizationEndpointTests`, `CoreCorrectnessTests`; independent SharePoint acceptance for edge ordering remains #18. |
| R02 | Outer errors now emit ErrorMessage, use `RequestNotSupported`, distinguish well-formed unsupported versions from HTTP syntax rejection, and map malformed decoded Cell payloads to Cell18 without exposing exception details. Wire/direct incompatible binary versions both return Cell15. | Implemented synthetic classification in `FssHttpTests`, `CellBinaryRequestExecutorTests`, `CoreCorrectnessTests`; operation-specific and independent reference response acceptance remains #18. |
| R03 | Structural preflight rejects duplicate request/subrequest tokens before dispatch in wire and direct paths. Duplicate predecessors return `InvalidRequestDependencyType` without throwing. Missing/forward/cyclic dependencies remain deterministic per wire order, and failed dependencies do not roll back earlier independent operations. | Implemented synthetic gates in `DependencyTests` and `CoreCorrectnessTests`; independent cycle/batching reference acceptance remains #18. |
| R04 | Binary parsing enforces request/user-agent/subrequest header shapes, required singleton fields, duplicate operation payload rejection, data-package ordering, and query/put singleton shapes before execution. Reserved bits and permitted request extensions remain accepted or preserved. | Implemented core malformed gates in `BoundedProtocolMutationTests` and existing codec suites; specialized optional-object and independent reference matrices remain #18/#34. |
| R05 | Specialized filter/knowledge and application graph interpretation need independent SharePoint vectors; optional full-data fallback is distinct from missing required behavior. Historical token profiles, staged uploads and continuation are unadvertised/unsupported. | Valid fallbacks versus missing features, #7/#23/#24/#26; native clients #19/#31/#5. |

No `Closes #18` claim follows from this matrix. Hashing acceptance is assessed
against #26 separately, with the evidence limits in [protocol hashing](protocol-hashing.md).

Sources in this ledger are Microsoft specifications. Local code and tests are
implementation evidence, not protocol authorities. Fixture locations are
recorded in [automated testing](automated-testing.md), [bounded validation](bounded-protocol-validation.md) and
[interoperability](interoperability.md); new synthetic tests below are not captures.

This table inventories all fourteen; each operation's attribute/error audit is a
separate completion gate.

| ID | Operation or subtype | Current behavior and evidence | Classification and next acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| O02 | Coauth JoinCoauthoring | Schema lease plus explicit coauthor membership. A genuine one-to-two membership change records a pending transition. `LockTransitionTests`. [Join rules][Ojoin]. | Implemented server behavior; desktop transition/reference qualification remains #29/#5. |
| O03 | Coauth RefreshCoauthoring | Refresh preserves a longer existing expiry; expired membership rejoins. Explicit owner-matching refresh promotes legacy state without membership fields. `LockTransitionPersistenceTests`. [Refresh rules][Orefresh]. | Implemented with documented migration policy; qualify reference traffic. |
| O04 | Coauth ExitCoauthoring | Removes caller's lease/membership and its mirrored editor entry. Leaves other clients intact. `FssHttpLockCoordinatorTests`, `LockTransitionTests`. | Existing strict absent-owner error needs product-specific/idempotency audit under #18/#29. |
| O05 | Coauth ConvertToExclusive | Requires active owned membership, matching schema and valid conversion inputs. Other schema owners block conversion; requested failure release removes only caller. `LockTransitionTests`. [Conversion rules][Oconvert]. | Implemented; source/reference fixtures and HTTP qualification remain #29. |
| O06 | Coauth MarkTransitionComplete | Valid owned member acknowledges the pending transition; repeated acknowledgement succeeds. It emits no CoauthStatus and requires no invented TransitionID request attribute. `LockTransitionTests`. [Mark rules][Omark]. | Implemented; qualify captured transition lifecycle under #29. |
| O07 | Coauth GetCoauthoringStatus / CheckLockAvailability | Status counts coauthors independently of editors-table readers; availability checks locks. `LockTransitionTests`, `FssHttpLockCoordinatorTests`. | Audit optional/required inputs separately; no membership requirement inferred for status. #18/#29. |
| O08 | SchemaLock GetLock / RefreshLock / ReleaseLock / CheckLockAvailability / ConvertToExclusive | Shared leases, exact actor ownership, schema identity and exclusive conversion. `FssHttpLockCoordinatorTests`. | Implemented profile behavior; audit normative timeout/default/error differences under #18/#29. |
| O09 | ExclusiveLock GetLock / RefreshLock / ReleaseLock / CheckLockAvailability | Exclusive lease and actor checks; ordinary GetLock emits no unrelated coauthor status/transition ID. `FssHttpLockCoordinatorTests`, `LockTransitionTests`. | Implemented profile behavior; remaining input/error audit #18. |
| O10 | ExclusiveLock ConvertToSchema / ConvertToSchemaJoinCoauth | Atomic exclusive-to-schema conversion. Only the Join variant creates membership and reports stable file transition identity. Existing foreign editor identity cannot be overwritten. `LockTransitionTests`. [Conversion rules][Otoschema]. | Implemented; full reference/desktop acceptance remains #29. |
| O11 | EditorsTable JoinEditingSession / LeaveEditingSession / RefreshEditingSession / UpdateEditorMetadata / RemoveEditorMetadata | Independent presence/metadata and knowledge advancement, actor-bound GUID editor keys. `EditorsTableStateTests`, authorization tests. | Audit every attribute, timeout and invalid metadata response under #18. |
| O12 | WhoAmI | Uses authenticated subject identity. `AuthorizationEndpointTests`, `LibraryAuthenticationTests`. | Implemented; schema/attribute audit #18. |
| O13 | ServerTime | Reports server time. `FssHttpTests`, `CapturedWordOpenTests`. | Implemented; response schema audit #18. |
| O14 | GetDocMetaInfo | Current document information. `MetadataVersioningResponseBuilderTests`. | Implemented current-state response; optional fields audit #18. |
| O15 | GetVersions | Servable immutable history entries with canonical authorized URLs. `MetadataVersioningResponseBuilderTests`, `RevisionHistoryTests`; merged #52/RH rows. | Implemented bounded history; independent history/reference and client gates remain #7. |
| O16 | AmIAlone | Stable file TransitionID required; true for one active coauthor without pending transition. Readers do not count. `LockTransitionTests`. | Pending suppressing true is a compatibility interpretation, documented below. Qualify #29/#5. |
| O17 | LockStatus | Reports current shared/exclusive/unlocked state and expiry. `FssHttpLockCoordinatorTests`. | Implemented; response attributes/error audit #18. |

| O19 | Versioning | GetVersionList and graph-aware RestoreVersion, merged #52. `RevisionHistoryTests`, `OuterDocumentOperationTests`. | Selected history subset implemented; token profiles/reference gates #7/#18. [Subtypes][Overops]. |

Whole-batch token preflight prevents earlier mutation on later duplicates, and duplicate predecessors return a dependency error. `DependencyTests`, `CoreCorrectnessTests`. [Dependency rules][Odependencies]. | Implemented bounded wire-order behavior; independent cycle/batching acceptance remains #18. |

Identity/routing tests, `AuthorizationEndpointTests`, `CoreCorrectnessTests`, and captured Word open. | Implemented selected behavior; independent edge acceptance remains #18. |

### Coordination persistence policy

`CoordinationState` stores coauthor membership and pending transition separately
from `EditorState`. Membership references a schema lease. Permission revocation
removes membership with its lease; expiry removes only expired membership. A
pending transition survives a drop from two members to one until acknowledgement;
complete teardown clears it. The file's ResourceID remains its TransitionID.
`LockTransitionPersistenceTests` exercises detached coordinators across persisted
transactions and old JSON without the additive fields. The PostgreSQL case is
opt-in in the full runner.

Older persisted editors-table entries alone do not prove coauthor membership.
An explicit owner-matching RefreshCoauthoring request promotes a legacy schema
owner into the new tracker. This is migration policy. It avoids silently treating
readers as coauthors. `AmIAlone` returns false while a transition remains pending;
this is a compatibility interpretation of notification behavior, not a literal
extra rule in the [AmIAlone operation][Oalone]. MS-SHDACCWS IsOnlyClient is a
separate operation and is not implemented here.

## Binary operations and request envelope

[The current normative request enumeration][Btypes] contains four operations.
Additional legacy enum values in `Enums.cs` do not establish a current requirement.

| ID | Wire requirement/applicability | Current behavior and evidence | Classification and next acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B01 | QueryAccess, type 1 | Reports read/write permission separately. `QueryAccessTests`, `AuthorizationTests`. | Implemented. |
| B02 | QueryChanges, type 2 | Mixed/repeated file, persisted opaque metadata and editor queries share independent knowledge/errors and a deduplicated package. Query-only inline groups can carry negotiated schema-1 hashes. `RepeatedQueryTests`, `MixedPartitionQueryTests`, `NegotiatedHashStorageTests`, Microsoft-client interoperability tests. | Selected query/metadata/hash behavior implemented; filter specialization/reference qualification remain #18/#23/#26. |
| B03 | PutChanges, type 5 | Graph-aware complete file and opaque metadata publication, coherency, requested applied indexes and durable receipts; merged #51. `FilePartitionSaveTests`, `MetadataPublicationTests`, `AppliedStorageIndexTests`. | Staging/alternate/buffered/coalescing modes remain unsupported; #27. |
| B04 | AllocateExtendedGuidRange, type 11 | Typed request/response codecs; fresh UUID namespace and exact count for 1..100000, with exclusive upper bound in 1000..100000. Requires write access; does not mutate content/state. `GuidAllocationTests`, independently decoded `GuidAllocationInteropTests`. [Allocation rules][Ballocate], [response bounds][Ballocresponse]. | Implemented #25; oversized/zero counts are an explicit local rejection policy. UUID namespaces avoid a persisted global counter; uniqueness has normal UUID collision probability. |
| B05 | Enum-only QueryKnowledge / QueryRawStorage / PutRawStorage / QueryDiagnosticStoreInfo | Explicitly unsupported. Not listed in the current normative four-operation enumeration. | No new required method inferred. Historic-profile applicability still needs audit #18. |
| B06 | UserAgent identity/version | GUID or strict UTF-8 ClientAndPlatform accepted; both accepted on read because simultaneous identity is SHOULD NOT, not MUST NOT. String form replaces GUID on output. Required version, duplicate/length/framing checks. `UserAgentTests`; captured no-UserAgent QueryAccess exception retained. [Request syntax][Breq]. | Compatibility defect #37 fixed; no inference of OneNote qualification. |
| B07 | Optional TargetPartitionId | Typed selector precedes operation data; malformed/duplicate/misplaced objects fail. Explicit known targets select their partition; absent targets inherit the SOAP selector, including after another operation used an explicit target. Unknown selectors get explicit unsupported errors. [Target partition][Btarget]. | Known routing implemented #23/#38. `TargetPartitionTests`, `TargetPartitionExecutionTests`, mixed Microsoft-client interoperability test. Unsupported partition writes remain explicit errors. |
| B08 | Subrequest ID / priority | IDs and priorities retained. Current execution uses wire order, allowed by SharePoint 2013 product behavior. [Subrequest rules][Bsubreq]. | Unique ID and range validation audit remains #18. Ascending priority required when selecting a profile without that exception. |
| B09 | RequestHashOptions | Typed schema 1, C/D and reserved-bit handling. Invalid negotiation rejects before execution. Chosen inline groups use ordered MS-PCCRC content information; P06-P10/H01-H04. Storage SHA-256 remains independent. | Implemented selected negotiated response facility #26; capture/client qualification remains distinct. |
| B10 | CellRoundtripOptions | Version-token/non-generic hints skipped. SharePoint 2013 product behavior lacks this extension. | Profile audit #18; version-token support #7 before a newer negotiated profile. |
| B11 | Reserved envelope/allocation fields | Allocation writer emits zero; reader ignores reserved byte. `AllocateExtendedGuidRangeTests`. | Other reserved fields and unknown-object handling still need field-level audit #18. |

## Query controls and knowledge

The [QueryChanges rules][Bquery] and [product notes][Bproducts] define the controls.
Code is in `QueryChangesSubRequestData`, `FileQueryResponseBuilder`,
`ClientKnowledge` and `QueryChangesResponseShaper`.

| ID | Control | Current behavior and evidence | Classification / gate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Q01 | AllowFragments / AllowFragments2 | Parsed as permission; no fragment response or continuation. Budget failures return explicit errors. `QueryChangesResponseShaperTests`. | Missing continuation #24; permission alone does not require fragmentation. |
| Q02 | IncludeFilteredOutDataElementsInKnowledge | Parsed and used when building returned knowledge. `BudgetAndQueryTests`, `RetentionAndKnowledgeTests`. | Implemented. |
| Q03 | RoundKnowledgeToWholeCellChanges | Whole cell retransmission when requested and any visible element is unknown. Knowledge tests. | Implemented. |
| Q04 | ReturnFileHash | Parsed; not emitted. SharePoint 2010/2013 product behavior ignores this control. | P12/H05 permitted fallback; byte-equivalent responses tested independently of data-element negotiation. Newer profiles remain unadvertised. |
| Q05 | UserContentEquivalentVersionOk / response equivalent-version bit | No equivalence-version model. | Profile/product audit #18; version behavior #7. |
| Q06 | IncludeStorageManifest / IncludeCellChanges / CellId | Actual mapped file cells selected from the index/manifest; multi-cell scopes use bounded current dependency closure, per-scope knowledge and waterline zero. `RepeatedQueryTests`, `InheritedFileGraphTests`. | Supported file scope subset #23; filter specialization and reference qualification remain. No invented wire root control. |
| Q07 | Data constraint | Exact serialized byte budgeting; no continuation when too small. `QueryChangesResponseShaperTests`. | Budget behavior implemented; continuation #24. Length measurement without temporary serialization is performance #33. |
| Q08 | QueryChangesVersioning | SP2010/2013 ignore decision under note17; Waterline is never a version token. `IgnoredQueryChangesVersioning` and `HistoryProfileFallbackTests` byte-equivalence; merged #52/RH04. | Valid selected-profile fallback. Higher token profile remains unadvertised #7. |
| Q09 | All / type / index-reference / cell / custom / ID / hierarchy filters | Parser skips specialization; full response by default; CellBridge returns an error when FailIfUnsupported permits failure. Filtered query tests. | Permitted full fallback; specialization is optional traffic reduction until a target client needs it. #23/#33. |
| Q10 | GUID/serial ranges and mapping knowledge | Authoritative mapping serials, conservative retransmission, bounded entries. Query response decoding retains the validated complete Knowledge bytes, preserving all serial/mapping ranges on reserialization; scalar fields remain a minimal summary. Clear `KnowledgeBytes` to deliberately rebuild from scalar fields. `ClientKnowledge`, `StorageIndexMappingSerials`, `QueryKnowledgeResponseTests`. | Implemented supported forms; no broader response-knowledge parsing claim. Unknown request knowledge forms use validated full-response fallback. |
| Q11 | Waterline, fragment, version-token and other knowledge forms | Waterline alone never suppresses bytes. Specialized forms need further audit. | Valid conservative fallback where permitted; fragment/version semantics depend on #24/#7. #18 tracks exact applicability. |

## Upload controls

[PutChanges][Bput] and [AdditionalFlags][Bputflags] define these fields. Actual
acceptance is in `FilePartitionSaveHandler`, not just the deserializer.

| ID | Control | Current behavior and evidence | Classification / gate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| W01 | Expected storage index / imply-null flag | Graph mapping coherency before atomic publication. File save tests. | Implemented supported file mode. |
| W02 | Partial / PartialLast | Explicit per-operation rejection before publication; independent queries and complete saves continue. `UploadHintTests`. | Missing staging #27. |
| W02a | MultiRequestPutHint | Accepted for complete file saves. This flag reduces automatic coalescing; it does not establish a staged transaction. Normal publication, locks, permissions and receipt retries apply. `UploadHintTests`, including PostgreSQL provider recreation. | Complete-save hint implemented #27; no claim of partial upload staging. |
| W03 | FavorCoherencyFailureOverNotFound | Flag permitted; audit exact error precedence. | Unresolved implementation evidence #18/#27. |
| W04 | AbortRemainingPutChangesOnFailure / ReturnCompleteKnowledgeIfPossible | Accepted without forcing new semantics; specification says server ignores these controls. | Required ignore behavior, not a missing feature. |
| W05 | LastWriterWinsOnNextChange | Explicitly rejected. | Missing alternate coherency #27. |
| W06 | ContentVersionCoherencyCheck / AuthorLogins / reserved byte | Opaque coherency bytes retained then ignored as specified; author strings retained, identity/access still comes from authenticated actor; reserved byte ignored on read. Put codec/save tests. | Field-specific historical applicability audit #18; no client-supplied permission identity. |
| W07 | ReturnAppliedStorageIndexIdEntries | Null applied-index ID when bit A is clear, matching the captured Word saves. With A set, the ID is returned but the requested index data is missing. `SaveAcknowledgementTests`. | Missing returned index data #18/#27; ordinary captured Word saves do not request it. |
| W08 | ReturnDataElementsAdded | Returns newly admitted unique IDs, including when bit B is clear, matching captured SharePoint Word acknowledgements. Retained and duplicate IDs are excluded; durable retries replay the stored acknowledgement. `SaveAcknowledgementTests`, captured HTTP save replay with independent decoder. | Implemented reference profile; Word's intermittent pending-save UI still needs desktop qualification. |
| W09 | CheckForIdReuse | Conflicting reused element IDs rejected before publication. Save tests. | Implemented supported retained graph. |
| W10 | CoherencyCheckOnlyAppliedIndexEntries | Explicit rejection. | Missing mode #27. |
| W11 | FullFileReplacePut | Explicit rejection; optional flag, absent in SharePoint 2013 behavior, and not required for a full save. | Optional/profile capability #18/#27; do not label ordinary full-file saves missing. |
| W12 | RequireStorageMappingsRooted | Explicit rejection. | Missing requested validation mode #27. |
| W13 | AdditionalFlags reserved bits/compact reserved field | Reserved high bits accepted; trailing bytes retained. Excel bit 15 regression passes. | Audit canonical reserved write and full malformed-field handling #18. |
| W14 | Lock ID / client knowledge / diagnostics | Lock token parsed and checked. Other optional trailing objects skipped; diagnostic requests are not diagnostic-store operations. | Codec/response applicability audit #18; knowledge optimization is conditional #33. |

## Graph and application partitions

[The abstract data model][Badm] defines cell-scoped objects, partitions and inherited
revisions. A schema adapter decides how opaque bytes become a usable application
file. The file adapter still validates its schema/root and reconstructed package.

| ID | Requirement | Current behavior and evidence | Classification / gate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| G01 | Multiple roots, cells and object partitions | Immutable generic graph plus format-2 durable capture/restore; file saves and scoped queries preserve cell/partition identity. `GenericGraphPersistenceTests`, `RepeatedQueryTests`. | File host integration implemented #20; general non-file publication and independent graph reference fixtures remain. |
| G02 | Nearest definitions through base revisions | Generic resolver supplies inherited file objects with nearest-definition precedence. Missing/cyclic required ancestry fails before publication; legacy self-contained file path retained. `InheritedFileGraphTests`. | File save/reopen and scoped query integration implemented #21; reference qualification remains. |
| G03 | Opaque objects, BLOB and cell references | Capture/restore retains immutable payload handles, mapping serials and BLOB closure. File publication resolves declared BLOBs by identity; scoped queries follow effective cell references and allow bounded cycles. `GenericGraphPersistenceTests`. | File flow and generic codec implemented #22; non-file publication and reference qualification remain. Automatic pruning stays disabled. |
| G04 | File reconstruction | `PartitionGraphSnapshot` adapts resolved partition-1 objects through `FilePartitionSaveHandler` with ZIP/content validation and graph identity. Metadata partitions cannot satisfy file references; never largest-BLOB recovery. File/replay tests. | Supported file adapter integration #20; no OneNote adapter implied. |
| G05 | Hash-prefixed/excluded-data object groups | Standalone query-only projection and typed hash/excluded records; `ObjectGroupWireHash`, `ProtocolHashingTests`. Materializable graph readers still reject these forms. | Implemented response subset #26, hash/excluded uploads/cache injection unsupported. |
| G06 | Application metadata | Existing index placeholder; no complete application metadata graph read/write. | Missing #28. |
| G07 | Native OneNote synchronization | No qualified discovery/open/edit/sync workflow or server adapter. Synthetic generic graph tests establish no client compatibility. | Missing target #19/#31, depends on graph, query and staging work. |
| G08 | Physical .one/.onetoc2 import/export or semantic preview | Separate file codecs/application features. | Outside the chosen desktop synchronization milestone. |

## Acceptance still required before completing #18

- Resolve the field-specific gates below, including missing Etag/timestamp
  enforcement, version/token validation, error spelling and dependency duplicates.
- Qualify every ignored extension's fallback and each operation's complete
  error precedence against sanitized reference traffic. Schema inventory does
  not itself prove execution coverage or permitted ignore behavior.
- Acquire reference fixtures for generic non-file graphs and partial staging.
  The new bounded cases are synthetic. No SharePoint reference notebook exists.
- Validate future discovery/profile changes against these rows. Keep version-14
  parsing distinct from negotiated version-14 feature support, and preserve
  explicit unsupported modes until the relevant gates pass.

Operational export/recovery/compaction is tracked by #32/#35/#3. Performance work
is tracked by #33. Fuzzing/differential validation is #34. These categories do not
become missing protocol methods just because they improve reliability or speed.

## Field audit conventions

The following inventory was checked against Microsoft's published schemas and
processing pages on 2026-10-05. `Schema required` and `schema optional` describe
presence, not a claim that an optional attribute's behavior can be ignored.
Explicit MUST, SHOULD and MAY conditions override that shorthand. Conditions
apply to outer 2.0 and binary 13/11 unless a row names another version. A client
MUST is distinguished from a server MUST. No version-14 capability set is
advertised. Product notes are exceptions for named products, not universal
protocol rules.

Evidence labels below link to the actual code and representative tests. A suite
link means related implementation evidence; it does not mean every condition in
that field's normative page has an acceptance test. `None` in the fixture column
means no reviewed reference fixture is mapped for that condition. `Open` gates
remain tracked by the listed issue, even when ordinary requests already work.
The existing stable V/O/B/Q/W/G identifiers above remain unchanged.

| Evidence | Code path | Tests | Reviewed reference fixture |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| P | [SOAP parser](../src/CellBridge.FssHttp/CellStorageRequestParser.cs), [models/writer](../src/CellBridge.FssHttp/CellStorageModels.cs) | [FssHttpTests](../tests/CellBridge.FssHttp.Tests/FssHttpTests.cs), [response URL tests](../tests/CellBridge.FssHttp.Tests/ResponseUrlEncodingTests.cs) | [Word open](../testdata/api/word-enable-editing.xml) has ordinary envelope values; no malformed-schema oracle. |
| I | [identity resolver](../src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/DocumentRequestResolver.cs) | [identity](../tests/CellBridge.FssHttp.Tests/DocumentIdentityTests.cs), [routing](../tests/CellBridge.Interop.Tests/IdentityRoutingTests.cs) | Word open plus [save-first](../testdata/sharepoint/fixtures/save_reopen/save-first.json); no unknown-ID reference error. |
| D | [dependency evaluator](../src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/FssHttpDependencies.cs) | [provider dependency tests](../tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/DependencyTests.cs), [Microsoft client tests](../tests/CellBridge.Interop.Tests/DependencyTests.cs) | Word open has Coauth/SchemaLock/Cell fallback; no duplicate-token reference. |
| C | [processor](../src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/CellBridgeRequestProcessor.cs), [durable service](../src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/CellBridgeDocumentService.cs) | [partition routing](../tests/CellBridge.FssHttp.Tests/CellPartitionSelectorTests.cs), [authorization](../tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/AuthorizationTests.cs) | Word open and save-first ordinary Cell attributes only. |
| L | [lock coordinator](../src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/FssHttpLockCoordinator.cs) | [lock transitions](../tests/CellBridge.FssHttp.Tests/LockTransitionTests.cs), [persistence](../tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/LockTransitionPersistenceTests.cs) | Word open lock lifecycle subset; conversion/coauthor reference gates remain. |
| E | [editor execution](../src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/CellBridgeRequestProcessor.cs), [editor state](../src/CellBridge.Storage/DocumentStore.cs) | [editor tests](../tests/CellBridge.FssHttp.Tests/EditorsTableStateTests.cs), [authorization](../tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/AuthorizationTests.cs) | [editors table](../testdata/sharepoint/fixtures/v13_11/editors-table-token-8.fsshttpb.b64); no full metadata-error matrix. |

### Discovery and profile boundaries

| ID | Normative source and applicability | Current behavior, code and test | Fixture and acceptance gate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| V05 | [MS-OCPROTO 2.1.2.1](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/office_protocols/ms-ocproto/cb028dd5-d8ca-4011-9513-e13ad52dfcf8) describes discovery; [Web server discovery 2.1.3.1](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/office_protocols/ms-ocproto/11f64ef0-e79d-42e7-93d7-94e8ef4ae670) routes clients among supported protocols. These overview sections do not mandate unrelated SharePoint APIs. | [Endpoints](../src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/CellBridgeEndpoints.cs) emit `X-MSFSSHTTP: 1.0`; [host integration tests](../tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/HostIntegrationTests.cs) cover configured HTTP hosting; a focused discovery marker assertion is still absent. This HTTP marker is distinct from SOAP 2.0 and binary 13/11. | Word open reference; client feature-activation qualification remains #18/#19/#31. |
| V06 | [MS-FSSHTTP version types 2.2.4.9](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/2d24cd6b-3a05-4846-924b-17ec519a62df), [major](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/a16d7460-1185-4016-9278-3f05df8400de), [minor](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/3e687206-040f-4d32-b829-5d30c5e98c03). Presence is required; minor is reserved. | P defaults missing values to 2/0. C does not establish a full incompatible-version response matrix. | Ordinary Word open; malformed/unsupported version acceptance absent #18. |
| V07 | [MS-FSSHTTPB 2.2.2][Breq]: protocol versions 12/13/14, minimum MUST 11. [Response 2.2.3](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttpb/00134396-bd4f-4d19-a238-62d872abac10) has matching version/signature rules. | B retains supplied versions without rejecting invalid values. The endpoint always writes 13/11, even after reading 14. Version 14 parsing is not negotiation. | [SharePoint13ProfileTests](../tests/CellBridge.FssHttpB.Tests/SharePoint13ProfileTests.cs), 13/11 fixtures; invalid-version/reference response gates #18. |
| V08 | [MS-FSSHTTP 2.3.3.1](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/6d98cb6b-11a7-4c8d-8fbe-5cf291e1c223): outer 2.0 client MUST use PartitionID and BypassLockID; 2.2+ changes where partition/lock identifiers are carried. | C accepts known binary targets as well as outer selectors. This is an additive compatibility path, not advertisement of outer 2.2. | [target execution](../tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/TargetPartitionExecutionTests.cs), ordinary file fixture; cross-version lock/selector matrix #18/#38. |

### Outer attributes

Each field is listed in its declaring schema. Inherited fields retain their
original row; the fourteen operation type values are listed separately below.
The generic union attribute groups do not remove operation-specific presence or
range requirements. C retains unknown `SubRequestData` attributes; retention
alone does not implement their semantics or validate their allowed operation.

### Outer elements and batching

A schema extension inherits the token/error fields in A rows. The table lists
children separately from attributes. All supported outer operations produce
one SubResponse per executed/evaluated SubRequest; publication is per supported
binary PutChanges operation, not an atomic transaction across the whole SOAP
collection. No future partial/history/deletion behavior is inferred here.

| ID | Element and normative source | Rule and applicability | Code/test evidence | Fixture / acceptance / issue |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| X001 | `Request` [2.2.3.2](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/91fb9fd5-ef2d-4484-b57c-f0aedf7d0f00) | Global element declaration; this is not a child with the same name. | P: Subset parsed/serialized; complete validation open. | P ordinary fixtures; cardinality/namespace acceptance #18. |
| X002 | `Request.GenericProperties` [2.2.3.2](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/91fb9fd5-ef2d-4484-b57c-f0aedf7d0f00) | Schema child; presence follows stated minOccurs. | P: Request generic properties discarded. | None; typed properties/presence applicability #18/#30. |
| X003 | `Request.SubRequest` [2.2.3.2](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/91fb9fd5-ef2d-4484-b57c-f0aedf7d0f00) | MUST one or more per Request. | P: Parser and direct processor reject an empty list before dispatch. | `FssHttpTests`, `CoreCorrectnessTests`; independent response acceptance #18. |
| X004 | `RequestCollection` [2.2.3.3](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/27d51e1e-3fd1-4087-b2a3-f882037370a7) | Global element declaration; this is not a child with the same name. | P: Subset parsed/serialized; complete validation open. | P ordinary fixtures; cardinality/namespace acceptance #18. |

| X090 | [Common sequencing 3.1.4.1](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/918c3440-7d05-4c3a-8d51-0bad627bdb23) | Server MUST match request/response and subrequest/subresponse; shared locking MUST support Coauth or SchemaLock; Coauth MUST track transition; expired leases MUST release. | C/L/D; durable leases and explicit transition membership. | Word open subset; concurrent distinct-desktop qualification #5/#29, authoritative shared leases #45. |
| X091 | [DependencyTypes 2.2.5.3][Odependencies] | Five defined conditions, not four; OnSuccessOrNotSupported is distinct from OnExecute. | D supports all five; checks previous responses in wire order. | Word fallback and Microsoft client tests; token cycles, duplicates and full truth table #18. |
| X092 | [Dependency error types 2.2.5.2](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/e72ec60a-bff7-4e1f-a1b5-a987854a5730) | Failed dependency MUST prevent its execution with the applicable error. This does not roll back earlier independent operations. | D/T: Missing/forward predecessors fail without execution; duplicate predecessors return `InvalidRequestDependencyType`; structural duplicate tokens reject the whole batch before dispatch. | `DependencyTests`, `CoreCorrectnessTests`; independent malformed/cycle acceptance #18/#34. |

### Every outer operation subtype

These values come from the normative simple types, independently of repository
enums. Attribute presence/range is in A rows; request/response/error condition
coverage remains an acceptance gate. The spec's subtype definition and its
processing page must both be used when their general and operation-specific
rules differ. A parser enum alone never proves implementation.

### Outer errors and enum-only distinctions

The schema error union includes generic, dependency, lock/coauthor, Cell, editors
and versioning types. Each value below is inventoried independently. `Not emitted`
means missing or inapplicable trigger coverage must be resolved; it does not
mean every deployment must manufacture this error. Checkout, IRM and cold storage
are conditional facilities. They are not unrelated APIs to add for enum coverage.

The implementation also emits `NotSupported` and recognizes
`SubRequestNotSupported`, while the normative generic enumeration names
`RequestNotSupported`. Treat those spellings as an unresolved wire compatibility
item. Do not count a successful OnNotSupported fallback as proof that every
error is schema-valid. Response-level `ErrorMessage` is required for non-success
Response errors and is currently absent from the model. SubResponse error detail
has separate rules; the ErrorCode requirement is not satisfied by an HRESULT alone.

### Binary request and response fields

Field presence and encoding come from the linked format section. `Layout` means
an encoding definition without a separate MUST/SHOULD/MAY statement for that
field. Reserved receive behavior and conditional requirements are called out.
Related suite evidence does not close missing scalar validation or reference
vectors. Synthetic mutations are tested with bounded inputs; arbitrary bit
changes are not assumed invalid.

| ID | Field and source | Normative/profile rule | Behavior and code/test evidence | Fixture / missing acceptance / issue |

### Negotiated hashing and versioning decision (#26)

Schema 13/11 does not by itself disable hashing. The selected configuration now
hashes complete inline query-only groups with nonempty content. See P06-P11 and
[protocol hashing](protocol-hashing.md) for selection, full-data fallback and
budget rules. Storage addresses and receipt digests remain independent. The
earlier zero-group audit decision is superseded for this bounded facility.

| ID | Normative source / applicability | Obligation | Current evidence / acceptance gate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H01 | [Request hashing options 2.2.2][Breq]; [product notes3/6/30/32][Bproducts] | Optional declaration; schema MUST be 1. Reserved bits ignored. SP2010 lacks the declaration; support is configuration-dependent, not categorically excluded by schema13. | `RequestHashOptions`, `ProtocolHashingOptions`, P06; both execution paths validate before operations. `ProtocolHashingTests`, `NegotiatedHashStorageTests`; #26. |
| H02 | [Query processing 3.1.4.2][Bhash] | Either C or D requires hashes for groups chosen for hashing. C requires excluded-data for every declaration in hashed groups. | Select complete inline/nonempty/query-only groups. BLOB/empty/save-shared groups stay full and unhashed. All C/D combinations, mixed partitions and save preflight tested; P07/P10/P11, #26. |
| H03 | [Hash construction and ordering 3.1.4.2][Bhash] | MUST hash only ordered concatenated Object Data binary items, comparing unsigned ExGuid value, GUID components then unsigned partition. | `ObjectGroupWireHash.ObjectOrder`; shuffled/unsigned/partition vectors and split-block tests in `ProtocolHashingTests`. No captured negotiation claimed; P08, #26. |
| H04 | [Data Element Hash 2.2.1.12.6.6](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttpb/d6f7ebd1-76ca-48ec-aeaa-18d2ef0157c0); [MS-PCCRC2.3](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/windows_protocols/ms-pccrc/36171657-7f15-419e-973b-6612b1799117) | Scheme MUST be1 and BinaryItem MUST contain v1 Content Information. SHA-256 generation is a selected allowed algorithm. | `PccrcContentInformation`, independent Python vectors following Microsoft layout with literal encoding/digests; 64-KiB/32-MiB bounds, separate immutable secret. No storage digest substitution; P09, #26. |
| H05 | [Return File Hash 2.2.2.1.3][Bquery], [response 2.2.3.1.2](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttpb/ddc826d3-48b5-4634-8e48-88ba7721bbab), [note13][Bproducts] | Client MAY request; server SHOULD return a file hash. SP2010/2013 ignores this flag. This differs from negotiated data-element hashing. | Typed request bit; current profile does not return file hash. Selected ignore fallback allowed; future higher profile needs independent file-hash schema/vector evidence. |
| H06 | [Query version selector 2.2.2.1.3][Bquery], [notes4/17][Bproducts] | Major/minor0.0 selects current only when selector supported. SP2010/2013 ignores selector and lacks Version Token Knowledge. Waterline is not a version token. | Merged #52/RH04 implements bounded ignore and byte-equivalent current response. Higher token profile and captured historical query remain open #7; P13. |

### Stream-object type inventory and unknown objects

The following inventory comes from the normative header tables, independently
of `Enums.cs`. Compound values specify whether the object needs a matching end.
An enum value is evidence of a codec identifier, not implemented execution.
`F` rows give nested fields and conditional gates; `G` execution applies only to
accepted generic graph records. Application-specific FSSHTTPD node records in
the file materializer are a separate referenced codec, not additions to these
tables.

This inventory records fields and objects, not exhaustive satisfaction of their
conditions. Primitive integer canonical forms, every reserved sender bit,
conditional error precedence, specialized filters/knowledge, captured hash negotiation
and server-reference matrices remain acceptance work under #18/#34 and their
child issues. Allowed profile fallbacks, optional performance facilities and
operational capabilities are not all missing mandatory implementations.


### Owner implementation review snapshots

These rows retain owner evidence at pinned heads before combined integration.
Pending-main and merge-gate labels describe those snapshots. The final section
records combined validation and the remaining qualification gates.

The history task (#7/#30) owns immutable revision snapshots, independent revision
numbers and the selected SP2010/2013 QueryChangesVersioning ignore fallback. The
uploads task (#27/#28) owns applied-index responses and application metadata
publication; staged Partial/PartialLast identity, abort/expiry and a reference
metadata notebook remain distinct gates. The external-host task (#44/#45/#46)
owns destination CAS/durable deduplication, publication delivery, shared locks,
authoritative lifecycle generation and migration/fencing. Its implementation in
[PR #50](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/pull/50), pinned at
`0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201`, merged into main as
[`55ca2c2b5d4841c575697d8ec31ddd2e8f4739ce`](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/commit/55ca2c2b5d4841c575697d8ec31ddd2e8f4739ce).
The reviewed contract requires fresh ResourceId and matching SOAP ResourceId for recreated
generations greater than one to prevent URL-only ABA. Pending owner PRs and their
combined validation remain separate gates. Merged source behavior is newer than
the published beta; pending behavior is not counted as release evidence. Shared
production interfaces are unchanged by this audit.

The following rows record PR #50 owner evidence at that pinned commit. Its
[feature guide](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/docs/external-host-reliability.md)
distinguishes host contracts from wire requirements: delivery journals, lifecycle
generations and destination CAS are implementation mechanisms, not additional
protocol fields mandated by the specifications.

| ID | Related normative obligation / host contract | Pinned implementation and evidence | Remaining gate |

External delivery is opt-in; when enabled its host contract requires atomic destination revision CAS and durable fingerprint-validated operation receipts. | [ExternalPublication.Append](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/src/CellBridge.Storage.Abstractions/ExternalPublication.cs) journals changed saves inside the document-service transition and retains immutable content pins; [ExternalRevisionPublisher](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/ExternalRevisionPublisher.cs) retries exact operations outside provider coordination. Common provider authority and commit fencing are host contracts enforcing those boundaries. | [SharedDocumentLocks](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/SharedDocumentLocks.cs) and [CoordinationFencing](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/src/CellBridge.Storage.Abstractions/CoordinationFencing.cs) check subject/token, lifecycle generation, authority epoch and provider-time expiry. Conditional tombstone/recreation and local eviction are separate host lifecycle contracts. | [DocumentLifecycle](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/src/CellBridge.Storage.Abstractions/DocumentLifecycle.cs) retains retired IDs and content, compares generation/state version, and requires a fresh ResourceId for explicit recreation. SOAP Cell/mutations on recreated paths require matching explicit identity. [HostLifecycleTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/HostLifecycleTests.cs) cover deletion/recreation races and retained pins; [owner acceptance](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/issues/46#issuecomment-5995881737). | Merged #50; combined namespace integration remains a gate #46. JSON remains additive format 2; PostgreSQL explicitly migrates schema 3 to 4 with hosts stopped, and old writers fail closed. External deletion/adoption and reference/client qualification remain separate. |

[PostgreSqlNamespaceLock](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/src/CellBridge.Storage.PostgreSql/PostgreSqlNamespaceLock.cs) precedes row/path locks; reservation checks use current snapshots after coordination and budget guards. [HostLifecycleTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/0284310d34cdd3d0b7be81da15b200231b656201/tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/HostLifecycleTests.cs) cover foreign retired aliases, namespace ordering and valid canonical generation-three recreation through ancestor tombstones. | Merged #50; joint validation against history rename remains a gate #30/#46. Aliases cannot be stolen, callback-cleared or fabricated by replacement state. This is not a generic SharePoint namespace API. |

The owner reports native Astra design/material/final approval and an isolated run
at this final commit with **all 19 checks passing: 698 .NET tests and 71 Python
checks, with two platform skips**, under ignored
`artifacts/host-reliability/full-5`. This is distinct from the pre-rebase audit's 683 .NET/71
Python isolated run. Shared scoped-receipt contract `27e4135`, file preservation,
original-fence and bounded-receipt changes `f7d7925`, and namespace/recreation
integration `0284310` are present in the host head; historical/upload operation
implementations were not imported. Joint rebase and combined provider/publication
validation with PR #51/#52 remain required before merge. No run establishes
OneNote, distinct-desktop or Office Online Server qualification. Issues
#44/#45/#46 remain open for the listed external gates.

The owner reports native Astra original design/material approval plus rebase
and exact-head approval, and **all 19 isolated checks passing: 736 .NET tests and
71 Python checks, with two platform skips**, under ignored
`artifacts/testing/upload-host-rebase/summary.json`. PR #51 is rebased onto merged
PR #50/main `55ca2c2`. This owner run validates **uploads plus merged host behavior
only**. It excludes historical restoration PR #52, whose rebase and combined
provider/publication tests remain a merge gate. It is distinct from this audit's
run; this documentation review does not independently certify those tests.

[MetadataPublicationTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/d9634148b6f578fba399036b28e6025682e0fc07/tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/MetadataPublicationTests.cs)
adds memory/PostgreSQL integration regressions for atomic file history/outbox
hooks, metadata preservation of a full blocked delivery queue and shared lock,
lifecycle/reservation and scoped-receipt state. Real deletion/recreation during
preparation and PostgreSQL process interruptions are covered. These tests do not
exercise PR #52 restoration or establish client qualification.

Partial/PartialLast, alternate coherency modes, buffered and coalescing metadata
writes remain explicit unsupported operations. Normative/reference association,
retry identity, abort and retention evidence is still required before defining
staging state or expiry. Issues #27/#28 remain open for those gates, SharePoint
reference metadata traffic and native OneNote/two-desktop qualification.

| ID | Normative condition / scope | Pinned implementation and evidence | Remaining gate |

| RH01 | [Get Version List 3.1.4.11.1](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/025134f4-f3a9-48df-a90c-8e0e07b30ae6) returns recent versions, with count chosen by server; [FileVersionDataType 2.3.1.43](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/3b1395c6-5f4c-4125-9efa-f8fb8bf5cf74) defines version metadata. | [RevisionHistory](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/src/CellBridge.Storage/RevisionHistory.cs) retains immutable file/application-metadata graphs, actor/time, resource/generation and independent revision numbers; metadata-only publications need not change file ContentVersion. Creation/legacy state seeds only known current history. [DocumentHistoryOperations](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/DocumentHistoryOperations.cs) supplies current-authorized listing and generation-bound historical downloads. [RevisionHistoryTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/RevisionHistoryTests.cs) and [RevisionHistoryInteropTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/tests/CellBridge.Interop.Tests/RevisionHistoryInteropTests.cs) cover retention, provider recreation and two-host access. | Pending main/combined publication integration #7/#30. Legacy `BuildGetVersions(StoredDocument, ...)` remains current-only with versioning disabled; the durable history path is separate. Default 1,000 history/restore-receipt caps reject admission without eviction. No invented pre-feature history or automatic pruning. |
| RH02 | [Restore Version 3.1.4.11.2](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/6e8d9f46-fa5a-46fc-a7e2-135c69c937f5) restores the specified version and returns VersionNotFound if unavailable; [revision manifest](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttpb/eb3351db-8626-4804-a35b-f3eeda13c74d) defines revision/root references. | [HistoricalGraphRestorer](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/src/CellBridge.FssHttpB/HistoricalGraphRestorer.cs) validates retained graph identities and creates fresh wrappers, selected indexes and mapping serials, preserving original history. DocumentHistoryOperations verifies selected historical content and rechecks current access, effective leases, lifecycle, expected history and partition generations before atomic publication; live security/editors/coordination survive. The reusable API has subject/generation-scoped keyed durable retries; SOAP correlation tokens do not establish retry identity, so each SOAP restore is a new operation. [HistoricalGraphRestorerTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/tests/CellBridge.FssHttpB.Tests/HistoricalGraphRestorerTests.cs) and [RestoreFencingTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/RestoreFencingTests.cs) cover inherited closure, races and lost replies. | Pending main and jointly validated history/metadata/delivery publication #7/#44/#45. Legacy inline-metadata restoration over a later opaque metadata graph remains explicitly unsupported. Independent SharePoint restore traces and desktop restore qualification remain open. |
| RH03 | [Rename input 2.3.1.33](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/cac57311-5f4f-41c8-aba5-1847da504926) requires a valid filename without relative path; [Properties input 2.3.1.52](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/openspecs/sharepoint_protocols/ms-fsshttp/a1f4efe8-dc76-4539-88d8-e10cbc8a0447) restricts PropertyIds to PropertyGet. | [OuterDocumentOperations](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/OuterDocumentOperations.cs) implements GetVersions/GetVersionList/RestoreVersion, identity-preserving Rename, and read-only PropertyEnumerate/PropertyGet. [MetadataVersioningResponseBuilder](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/src/CellBridge.AspNetCore/MetadataVersioningResponseBuilder.cs) emits version/property data. Released decoded path keys are permanently reserved to their resource; providers own the reservations and PostgreSQL takes the namespace guard before row/path locks. [OuterDocumentOperationTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/OuterDocumentOperationTests.cs) and [PathRetirementTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/PathRetirementTests.cs) cover wire schemas, authorization, escaping and namespace races. | Pending main and combined namespace/recreation tests with EH04 #30/#46. Default 128 reserved-name cap rejects admission without eviction. Other suboperations remain explicit unsupported; no lists/search/arbitrary property storage. Independent SharePoint version/rename/property traces remain open. |
| RH04 | [MS-FSSHTTPB Appendix B notes4/17][Bproducts] excludes Version Token Knowledge for SP2010/2013 and ignores QueryChangesVersioning. | [Query codec](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/src/CellBridge.FssHttpB/FsshttpbCellRequest.cs) bounds/skips the extension and records `IgnoredQueryChangesVersioning`. [HistoryProfileFallbackTests](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/blob/de476bf460aef324911a45a95242a02957fae6f1/tests/CellBridge.Storage.Tests/HistoryProfileFallbackTests.cs) checks byte-equivalent current responses with and without the extension. Legacy Waterline version-selector serialization still rejects. | Pending main evidence for Q08/H06. Higher-profile Version Token Knowledge and historical binary selectors, independent token fixtures and SharePoint traces remain open #7. No Waterline-to-token substitution. |

The history owner reports native Astra design/material/final-head approval and an
isolated **704 .NET tests and 71 Python checks passing, with two platform skips**,
under ignored `artifacts/history/history-final-3/summary.json`. History excludes
borrowed W07/application-metadata operation implementations and tests, retaining
agreed additive contracts/helpers and the minimal empty-index reader. The additive
format-2 codec reads old state, but all writers must upgrade together: older
writers can discard unknown fields, so rolling downgrade is unsupported. Public
beta constructors and legacy receipt defaults remain available; these PRs are
not included in the published beta packages.

PR #50 is merged and PR #51 has owner validation against that merged base.
PR #52 remains separate. Its joint rebase and combined provider/publication,
scoped-receipt, content-reference, lifecycle, namespace and host-lock validation
remain a merge gate. No validation combining all three owner implementations
is claimed by this ledger update. Issues #7/#30 remain open, and no independent
SharePoint history/property/rename or metadata traces, native OneNote,
two-desktop coauthoring or Office Online Server qualification is established.


### Audit validation on merged host base

The audit PR is rebased onto merged PR #50/main
`55ca2c2b5d4841c575697d8ec31ddd2e8f4739ce`. The four audit commits reapplied
without conflicts and their bounded assertions remain unchanged. The full
isolated runner passed **all 19 checks: 710 .NET tests and 71 Python checks,
with two platform skips**, under ignored
`artifacts/testing/audit-host-rebase/summary.json`; its own disposable AppHost
stopped successfully. This run includes merged host behavior and the audit
suites, but excludes pending PR #51 uploads and PR #52 historical restoration.
It supplements the earlier release-base audit run and does not establish
all-owner combined validation or new desktop qualification.


### Combined implementation validation

PRs [#50](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/pull/50),
[#51](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/pull/51) and
[#52](https://github.com/PatrickMatthiesen/cellbridge/pull/52) have completed
combined implementation validation. The full isolated runner passed 789 .NET tests and 71 Python
checks, with two Python platform skips. This includes PostgreSQL, provider
recreation, process-death recovery and two-host HTTP checks. The reviewed tree
includes the audit tests in PR #49; raw results remain ignored under
`artifacts/testing/parallel-integration-final-3`.

`CombinedPublicationTests` verifies complete metadata uploads, file saves, historical
restoration and queued external publication share coherent publication boundaries. It checks
partition-scoped exact retry acknowledgements, preservation of opaque metadata,
history/outbox deduplication, content pins, quota rollback and deletion/recreation
followed by saves and case-only rename on both providers.
`HistoryLifecycleIntegrationTests` checks clean replacement history, retired aliases
and matching resource identity for recreated SOAP mutations. These checks resolve
the combined implementation merge gates recorded in the owner snapshots above.

The source changes are separate from the published `0.1.0-beta.1` packages. True
Partial/PartialLast staging, continuation #24, captured hash negotiation qualification,
live graph pruning #3 and provider portability/migration tooling #32 remain open.
Legacy inline-metadata restoration over a later opaque metadata graph remains
explicitly unsupported. Independent SharePoint metadata/history/rename/property
traces, real WopiHost destination-contract adoption, native OneNote, two-desktop
coauthoring and Office Online Server qualification remain open.
